It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent, recognisable identity (green accent, black headlines, logo footer) and a logical route from company overview through AI factories, platform, networking, roadmap, markets and financials, and many headline-and-subhead slides state the point clearly (9, 16, 26). Several pages have real craft, such as the networking curve on slide 9 and the bar charts on slide 26. Consequential weaknesses hold it at competent: slide 2 is an unreadable wall of disclaimer text on the second page, slide 3 has tiny text over a busy photo, and many body slides (5, 7, 10, 14, 18, 20) are small, dense paragraphs with little hierarchy. Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent green and black identity, the headline plus green subhead pattern, the slide 9 scaling curve with its labeled lines, and the clean branded cover on slide 1.

**Most useful repair:** Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**What would change the score:** Redraw slides 16 and 26 so the visuals show the proportion and the trend directly, for example scaled icons or a bar for the power drop, and separate H1 FY25 from full-year bars with a simpler margin treatment.

_Confidence high. 32 of 32 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a forward-looking statements disclaimer and a non-GAAP notice, then a company overview stating NVIDIA's installed base and developer reach. It moves through the GPU's history, the shift to accelerated computing, and the idea of AI factories as a new class of data center. Core sections cover the full-stack platform, networking for scale-up and scale-out, CUDA libraries with speedup figures, energy efficiency per token, and scaling laws for training and inference. A roadmap slide sets out a one-year product cadence, followed by market reach across industries, enterprise and industrial AI adoption, and sovereign AI partners. The financial section shows revenue, operating income and margin, cash flow, capital allocation, and a market platform breakdown, with reconciliation tables in the appendix. The closing slides are reconciliation tables. Slides 5, 6 and 14 are good examples of headline-led framing, and slides 9, 16 and 26 show how charts carry the argument.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the NVIDIA investor presentation from October 2024 have?**

The NVIDIA investor presentation has 32 pages, and slide 1 is the title slide reading "NVIDIA Investor Presentation October 2024." Slide 2 contains the forward-looking statements disclaimer and the non-GAAP notice.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about the reach of its installed base and developers?**

Slide 3 says NVIDIA's platform is installed in several hundred million computers, is available in every cloud and from every server maker, and powers over 75% of the TOP500 supercomputers. It also says NVIDIA has close to 5.5 million developers.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about energy efficiency per token on slide 11?**

Slide 11 states that LLM energy efficiency improved 100,000X in the past 10 years. For GPT-MoE-1.8T energy per token, it lists Kepler at 42000 Joules/Token and Blackwell at 0.4 J/Token.

**What does the NVIDIA deck say about its one year product rhythm and roadmap?**

Slide 14 says NVIDIA's newest architecture, Blackwell, is an AI infrastructure platform that integrates seven chips, and that NVIDIA can update the entire AI infrastructure on a One Year Rhythm. Slide 16 adds that for a GPT-MoE-1.8T training workload, GB200 NVL72 gives a 4X reduction in power compared with Hopper.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract 3D illustration of stacked, curved green ribbon-like layers sweeping from the lower right toward the upper left. The layers are glossy lime-green (#8CC800 to #76B900 range) with dark forest-green (#1E4A0C) shadowed recesses in the lower right where the layers curve inward into a spiral opening. Thin pale light lines run diagonally across the upper left, fading into white. The composition is framed with the spiral mass occupying the right and bottom half, and the upper left is white and open. Lighting is soft, from the upper left, producing bright highlights along the top edges of the ribbons. The image is not cropped in a boxed sense; it fills the whole slide. One logo: the NVIDIA logo (green eye emblem #76B900 followed by black NVIDIA wordmark #000000) in the top-left corner.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Review: Consequential weaknesses hold it at competent: slide 2 is an unreadable wall of disclaimer text on the second page, slide 3 has tiny text over a busy photo, and many body slides (5, 7, 10, 14, 18, 20) are small, dense paragraphs with little hierarchy.

**Text**

NVIDIA
NVIDIA’s invention of the GPU in 1999 sparked the growth of the PC gaming market, redefined computer graphics, revolutionized accelerated computing, ignited the era of modern AI, and is fueling industrial digitalization across markets.
Today, two transitions are occurring simultaneously—accelerated computing and generative AI—transforming the computer industry and every other industry worldwide, and NVIDIA is enabling these transitions with our full-stack computing platform and data-center-scale offerings.
NVIDIA’s platform is installed in several hundred million computers, is available in every cloud and from every server maker, powers over 75% of the TOP500 supercomputers, and has close to 5.5 million developers.
Headquarters: Santa Clara, CA | Headcount: 30,000+

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph covers the entire slide with no margins. A single text column sits in the lower right, starting at about 61.7% from the left, with the bold title NVIDIA above the body paragraphs and the green headquarters line at the bottom. All text is left-aligned in that column. There is no separate title placeholder, footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of the NVIDIA headquarters campus, shown from an elevated angle. A large white steel lattice canopy with integrated solar panels spans the top of the frame diagonally. Below it, a curved glass-walled office building is on the left, two white-railed wooden elevated rings and a white walkway bridge cross the centre and right, and dense green trees, lawns, paved paths and wooden benches fill the lower half. Two people walk on the paved path near the centre, and a black umbrella with tables and chairs sits at the lower right of centre. Lighting is warm low-angle sunset sunlight with a clear pale-blue sky at top left. Dominant colours are white, green, warm amber and pale blue. The photo is not cropped, boxed or tinted, and no overlay is visible; the text sits directly on the foliage and path area at lower right.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph over a light grey #F2F2F2 layer on a white #FFFFFF base. Slide background is white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F2F2F2 layer beneath the photograph, which covers the whole slide. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF; the headquarters line is NVIDIA green #76B900. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans, a geometric humanist sans-serif. The title is bold, about 4.07% of slide height; body text is regular, about 1.48% of slide height; the headquarters line is regular, about 1.73% of slide height. Text lines are set with tight leading of about 1.6% of slide height. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or gradients are visible.

### Slide 4

**Text**

World’s First GPU Turns 25 / NVIDIA released GeForce 256, the world’s first GPU, 25 years ago. / This marked a milestone in computing by providing gamers with / higher frame rates, smoother gameplay, and life-like graphics. / NVIDIA's GPU set the foundation for the big bang of modern AI, / a decade later. The parallel processing power of NVIDIA GPUs and / the invention of a revolutionary programming model “CUDA” / attracted researchers who recognized the opportunity to / accelerate computationally-demanding applications like physical / and chemical simulations and deep learning. / NVIDIA released the GeForce RTX series in 2018, reinventing / computer graphics once again with real-time ray tracing and neural / rendering, applying AI to generate pixels at quality and speed no / one thought possible. / Today, the world recognizes GPUs not only for reshaping gaming / but also for powering the AI industrial revolution. NVIDIA GeForce / 256 started an era where gaming, computing, and AI evolve / together, fundamentally advancing entertainment, technology, / and society. / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Two-column split layout: a full-height photograph fills the left 44.9% of the slide, and the text column sits on the right from x 52.5% to about 92%. The title sits top-right at y 17.3%, left-aligned with the body, and a thin horizontal rule runs under it at y 27.6%. The body text starts at y 34.2% and runs to about 83%. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right at about x 93% and y 95%, with no page number and no footer text.

**Visuals**

Photograph of Times Square, New York, seen from street level, looking up at an angle. A tall blue-glass skyscraper rises through the upper left, with a pale cloudy sky behind it. The left side holds a large billboard reading “BEAUTIFULLY CRAFTED VISION” on a black-and-white image of a rock or wave, and a red vertical sign with partially visible lettering. A curved green billboard in the centre shows a close-up of a man's face covered in small square tiles, with the text “World’s First GPU Turns 25”, “2024” and an NVIDIA logo; a Nasdaq logo sits below it. Beside it is a narrow vertical green billboard reading “GeForce 256 / The World's First GPU Turns 25 / Inspiring what's next since 1999” with a column of years from 1999 to about 2015 and an NVIDIA logo at the bottom. Lower in the frame are a red-and-black “ALL NIGHT” LED sign and a red, white and blue LED American flag on a kiosk. Lighting is daylight; dominant colours are blue glass, green, white sky, red and grey. The photo is full-height and cropped at its right edge at x 44.9%, with no border. A light grey #F2F2F2 tint at 61% opacity sits behind the photo's left area.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide, with the photograph on the left panel. Plain white slide with a single photograph in a left panel. Title in NVIDIASans Bold, black #000000, about 4.4% of slide height. Body in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 2.2% of slide height, with paragraphs separated by roughly one line of space. Accent is NVIDIA green #76B900 used in the logo and the bottom-right bar. Sans-serif typefaces, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners. The horizontal rule is a thin mid-grey #8C8C8C line, about 1 px.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

NVIDIA Scaling with AI Scale-Up and Scale-Out / NVIDIA’s chips and systems supply chain expanded significantly in 2024, while demand continues to exceed supply. / NVIDIA Blackwell platform in full production – expanding to support x86 and NVIDIA Grace, air and liquid cooling, / NVLink 8-GPU to NVLink 72-GPU, InfiniBand and Ethernet, CSP, regional AI factories, and enterprise platforms. / One Year Rhythm at full infrastructure scale. / AI training continues AI scaling law. OpenAI o1 starts inference-time scaling law. Inference is driving significant / workload growth and demand. / Regional and sovereign clouds start up around the world. / NVIDIA AI Enterprise and Omniverse set the foundations for enterprise and industrial AI.

**Layout**

The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at y 94.8 percent with a green vertical bar at the far right edge. There is no slide number and no footer text.

**Visuals**

Diagram (bar and curve chart, no axes labels or numeric scale). (2) A dashed blue #0070C0 curve starting flat at the top of the blue block and falling to the right, ending near the lower middle of the chart, labelled 'General-Purpose / Computing' above it. (5) A tall solid green #76B900 shape on the right with a sloped top edge rising from left to right, split by a diagonal white stripe into an upper block labelled 'AI Factories' in white and a lower block labelled 'GPU-Accelerated / Data Centers' in white. (6) A thin grey horizontal baseline under all shapes. The four lines do not connect to the blocks with arrows; the curves cross each other. ### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

AI Factories—A New
Class of Data Centers
Production of digital intelligence tokens
AI factories are a new form of computing infrastructure. Its
purpose is not to store user and company data or run ERP and CRM
applications. AI factories are highly optimized systems purpose-
built to process raw data, refine it into models, and produce
monetizable tokens with great scale and efficiency.
In the AI industrial revolution, data is the raw material, tokens are
the new commodity, and NVIDIA is the token generator in the AI
factory.
Data
Tokens
Every company will produce digital intelligence. Tokens will be
transformed into intelligent responses and actions of digital
nurses, tutors, customer service agents, and chip designers,
manufacturing robots and autonomous cars, and weather
prediction agents to warn us of storms. Some companies will build
and operate AI factories, while others will rent.
AI Factory
Countries are awakening to the need to treat its data as a national
resource and AI factories as an essential national infrastructure.
Data encodes a nation's history, knowledge, and culture, and can be
transformed into the sovereign AI for its companies, startups,
universities and governments.
NVIDIA builds the complete AI system and licenses NVIDIA AI
Enterprise, the AI stack and operating system for AI factories.

**Layout**

Two-column split with a white left column (x about 0 to 55%) and a light grey right panel (x about 55 to 100%, full height). In the left column the title sits top-left at x about 7.3%, y about 8%, followed by a green subtitle and a thin horizontal rule at y about 27.6% spanning to x about 47%; the body text is left-aligned at x about 7.4% from y about 34% to 90%. The right panel holds a centred diagram (AI Factory box with Data input arrow and Tokens output arrow) between y about 33% and 65%. There is no footer and no slide number; the NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left at y about 95%.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a rounded rectangle outline (black stroke, about 1 px, corner radius about 2% of slide width) labelled AI Factory on its bottom edge, where the outline is broken by a gap around the label. Inside the box, from top to bottom: a green neural-network icon (a node cluster of 1 central and 6 outer circles connected by lines) centred at about x 77.5%, y 44%; and three green server-rack icons side by side at x 72.5%, 75.3% and 78.1%, each about 4.6% wide and 8.2% tall, centred at y about 54%. A black arrow runs from the text Data (left of the box) into the box's left edge at y about 49%. A black arrow runs from the box's right edge out to the text Tokens at y about 49%. Both arrows are simple lines with a filled triangular head, about 1 px. No chart, table or photograph. Colours: green #76B900 for icons; black #000000 for outlines, arrows and labels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the left, with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel at about 61% opacity (reads as about #F7F7F7) covering the right side.

### Slide 8

**Text**

NVIDIA’s Accelerated
Computing Platform
Data center scale innovation across chips,
networking, systems, software, and algorithms
NVIDIA has accelerated software and compute
by a 1,000,000X in the last decade, far
surpassing Moore’s law.
Accelerated computing requires full-stack
innovation—optimizing across every layer
of computing—from chips and systems to
software and algorithms, demanding deep
understanding of the problem domain.
Our platform extends from the cloud and
enterprise data centers to supercomputing,
edge computing, PCs, and robotics.

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a light grey field: a large diagram cluster occupies the left roughly 60% of the slide, and a left-aligned text column starts at x 67.1% with its title top at y 19.6%, roughly level with the top of the software-stack graphic. The text column runs to about y 78% with a generous right margin of about 5%; no footer, slide number, or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Diagram of the NVIDIA accelerated computing stack, all rendered as photographic product or software images on a light grey background, arranged in rows. (1) Top left: a horizontal strip of ten rounded-square CUDA-X library thumbnails at x 11.6 to 33.4%, y 21.3 to 26.9%, each showing a different colourful scientific or software visualisation (blue data-grid, blue ocean-like wave, purple and green simulation, green cube wireframe, purple molecule, orange and teal rendering, green terrain, grey vehicle-like render, and a dark blue app window). (2) Below it: four stacked full-width black bars with thin white labels, from top: “CUDA • DOCA • NCCL”, “Cluster-Scale Software”, “System Software”, “Chip Software”, spanning x 11.6 to 33.4%, y 28.9 to 37.8%. (3) Top centre: a dark rectangular screen at x 39.9 to 48.7%, y 28.4 to 37.7%, showing a close-up portrait of a man’s face inside a green-outlined box on the left and a small cluster of green and white node diagram on the right, above which sit three dark hexagonal icons with green bases at x 41 to 49%, y 25 to 29%. (4) Top right: a grey-framed screen at x 50.7 to 59.5%, y 28.5 to 37.5%, showing a grey 3D render of a white plaza with small humanoid robots, with a dark side panel. (5) Centre left: a block of ten tall black server racks in a row at x 8.7 to 37.0%, y 40.9 to 60.6%, each with fine green-yellow edge lighting, forming the GB200 NVL72 SuperPOD. (6) Centre: a tall narrow black switch and spine module at x 39.7 to 42.2%, y 44.9 to 60.4%, with grey-white cable bundles on its front. (7) Centre right: a bundle of light blue-grey cable ends curving downward at x 45.4 to 48.4%, y 48.0 to 55.5%, labelled Transceivers & Cables. (8) Right of that: a dark grey NVLink Switch box with cross-shaped green-teal connectors at x 51.9 to 58.5%, y 48.2 to 54.8%. (9) Bottom left: a dark Blackwell HGX-Node board with gold-coloured heatsinks at x 8.0 to 11.6%, y 70.2 to 77.3%. (10) Bottom left-centre: a tall dark Grace Blackwell MGX-Node chassis at x 13.2 to 17.3%, y 65.3 to 77.3%. (11) A row of seven square chip packages with gold or grey heat-spreaders, each about 3.5 to 4.5% wide, at x 18.7 to 48.7%, y 70.5 to 75.4%, labelled Chips Purpose-Built for AI Supercomputing. (12) Lower right: a tall dark two-socket compute unit with two gold chips at x 50.3 to 54.4%, y 65.2 to 77.6%, and a horizontal dark grey Quantum/Spectrum switch unit at x 55.8 to 61.8%, y 65.5 to 71.6%. Lighting is soft and diffuse from above, with each object casting a soft grey drop shadow toward the lower right. Framing is a flat, slightly elevated, full-slide arrangement with nothing cropped; no overlays or tints apply.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey #F0F0F0 with no gradient or texture. Title text is black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, sentence case, approximately 3.7% of slide height, left aligned, with tight leading. Subtitle is NVIDIA green #76B900 in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.5% of slide height, with the same left alignment. Body text is black #000000 in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.2% of slide height, with about 3.2% line spacing and blank lines between the three paragraphs. Diagram labels are NVIDIASans Medium about 1.2% of slide height in black #000000, with white #FFFFFF labels inside the black software bars. Shapes: the four software-stack bars are black #000000 rectangles with square corners and no visible outline. Thumbnails have small rounded corners. Product images have soft diffuse grey drop shadows to the lower right. No gradients on text or shapes.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A scatter-style chart plots GPU count against AI performance to show where InfiniBand, NVLink and Ethernet each fit, so the networking argument is carried by the visual.
- Review: Several pages have real craft, such as the networking curve on slide 9 and the bar charts on slide 26.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent green and black identity, the headline plus green subhead pattern, the slide 9 scaling curve with its labeled lines, and the clean branded cover on slide 1.

**Layout**

Centered two-line black bold title with a centered green subtitle across the top of the slide. Below, two equal columns with about 4.5% side margins: left, a light grey bordered line-chart box with a centered two-line caption beneath it; right, a dark photograph panel with a black three-column text band at its base. NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a green bar at the right edge; no page number is visible.

**Visuals**

Line chart in the left box titled none, with the Y axis labelled AI Performance (no units, no tick values, no range printed) and the X axis labelled # of GPU in a Data Center on a log-style scale with ticks 10, 100, 1k, 10k, 100k, 1M+ (no numeric values printed). Series: Traditional Ethernet, solid grey #8C8C8C curve from about 10 to about 10k, rising steeply then flattening (about). NVIDIA Spectrum-X AI Ethernet Fabric, solid green #76B900 curve from about 10 to about 100k (about). InfiniBand, solid teal #13826B curve from about 1k to about 100k, flattening above the Spectrum-X curve (about). NVLink + InfiniBand / Spectrum-X, light green #76B900 solid line from about 10 to about 1k, then dashed continuing up to an arrowhead pointing upper right; no legend box and no highlighted data points. Right: photograph of rows of data-centre server racks in dark aisles with blue and green lit cables and switches, viewed head-on in one-point perspective, two aisle blocks split by a dark central gap; cropped by the panel edges and overlaid by #151617 at 61% opacity; dark, low-key lighting with small green and blue LED accents; dominant colours near-black #151617, grey #3A3A3A, green #76B900, blue #3E8FB0. Three column text blocks sit on a solid black band. NVIDIA logo bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Solid white background. Title black #000000 in NVIDIASans Bold, about 4.4% of slide height, centred. Subtitle green #76B900 in NVIDIASans Regular, about 3.0%. ### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

Accelerated Computing / Starts with CUDA Libraries / Delivering up to 200X Speedup / Across Major Workloads / Unlike CPU general-purpose computing, GPU-accelerated computing requires software and algorithms to be re-designed. Software is not automatically accelerated in the presence of a GPU or accelerator. / NVIDIA CUDA libraries encapsulate NVIDIA-engineered algorithms that enable applications to be accelerated on NVIDIA’s installed base.  They deliver dramatically higher performance—compared to CPU-only alternatives—across application domains, including AI and high-performance computing significantly reducing run time, cost, energy, while increasing scale. / With over 400 CUDA libraires NVIDIA can address many major workloads across a wide range of industries. As new libraries become available, they unlock new markets adding to our long-term opportunity. / ~200X / Data Processing / cuVS, cuDF-Spark, cuDF-pandas, cuDF-Polars, cuGraph, cuML, XGBoost, RAPIDS, NeMo Curator, cuSOLVER, cuIO / ~200X / Computer Vision / CV-CUDA, Deepstream, TAO, Holoscan, cuCIM, TensorRT, Triton Inference Server, DALI, nvImageCodec, cuDNN, nvJPEG, nvJPEG2000, nvTIFF, NPP, Video Codec SDK, Magnum IO, NCCL, cuVS, DALI / ~100X / Science / Earth-2 CorrDiff, Holoscan, Parabricks, Monai, Modulus, Warp, cuLitho, cuQuantum, CUDA-Q, AmgX, cuDSS, cuFFT, cuSOLVER, cuBLAS, cuSPARSE, cuTENSOR, cuGraph, Magnum IO, NCCL, NVSHMEM, RAFT, cuNumeric, Sionna / ~100X / Deep Learning / cuDNN, CUTLASS, Megatron, TensorRT, TRT LLM, NCCL, NV-Triton, CUDA-optimized PyTorch, Tensorflow, Triton, Jax / ~50X / Recommender Systems / Merlin, HugeCTR, TensorRT, Triton Inference Server, cuBLAS, cuDNN, cuFFT, cuSPARSE, CUTLASS, Magnum IO, NCCL, cuVS / ~30X / Speech AI / Riva, TensorRT, Triton Inference Server, NeMo, cuBLAS, cuDNN, cuFFT, CUTLASS / ~100X / Agentic & Physical AI / ACE, Riva, Nemo, Tokkio Digital Human, Holoscan, Metropolis, Omniverse, Isaac, DRIVE, cuLitho, cuMotion, cuOpt, Aerial CUDA-accelerated RAN, Sionna, fVDB, PhysX, Warp, NVblox

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: the left column (about 0 to 47 percent of the width) holds the title, green subtitle, a thin horizontal rule and three body paragraphs, all left-aligned to a left margin at about 7.3 percent. The right column (about 57 to 91 percent of the width) stacks seven equal-width grey stat panels vertically, each with a large green multiplier on the left and a black category title with its library list on the right. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom-left with a green bar at the left edge; there is no slide number or footer text.

**Visuals**

Seven stacked rectangular stat panels, each a light grey (#F7F7F7) box with a thin light grey border, no shadow, square corners, arranged top to bottom: (1) '~200X' Data Processing; (2) '~200X' Computer Vision; (3) '~100X' Science; (4) '~100X' Deep Learning; (5) '~50X' Recommender Systems; (6) '~30X' Speech AI; (7) '~100X' Agentic & Physical AI. Each multiplier is set in bold-medium bright green (#76B900) at about 4.1 percent of slide height, with a small tilde prefix. No photographs, charts or icons. A thin grey horizontal rule (about #8C8C8C, roughly 0.2 percent of slide height thick) runs from the left edge to about 47 percent of the width below the subtitle. Small NVIDIA wordmark logo in black at bottom-left. A small green vertical bar at the bottom-left edge, about #76B900.

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF solid background.

- body at x 7.3, y 34.2, w 39, h 35.5 [NVIDIASans Regular; font size 2.22% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: Unlike CPU general-purpose computing, GPU-accelerated computing requires software and algorithms to be re-designed. / Software is not automatically accelerated in the presence of a GPU or accelerator. / NVIDIA CUDA libraries encapsulate NVIDIA-engineered algorithms that enable applications to be accelerated on NVIDIA’s installed base. They deliver dramatically higher performance—compared to CPU-only alternatives—across application domains, including AI and high-performance computing significantly reducing run time, cost, energy, while increasing scale. / With over 400 CUDA libraires NVIDIA can address many major workloads across a wide range of industries. As new libraries become available, they unlock new markets adding to our long-term opportunity. (Three paragraphs, line pitch about 2.4 percent of slide height, paragraph gap about 1.5 percent; no bullets)

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.
- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

Accelerated Computing Is Sustainable Computing
Order-of-Magnitude More Energy-Efficient
Accelerated computing requires higher peak power consumption than CPUs, however, completes workloads significantly faster and consumes less total energy
Energy Usage in AI Inference
Server TDP (kW)
NVIDIA
CPU
Time(sec)
Accelerated computing enables full-stack optimization from algorithm to GPU architecture, such as Tensor Core Transformer Engine; LLM energy-efficiency improved 100,000X in the past 10 years

**Layout**

Centred title block at top with the title on one line and a green subtitle beneath it, followed by two equal side-by-side panels with 4.5% side margins and a 23.6% top edge spanning 58.4% of the height. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right beside a thin green vertical bar at the right edge; no slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a clustered bar chart titled 'Energy Usage in AI Inference' with vertical axis labelled 'Server TDP (kW)' and horizontal axis labelled 'Time(sec)'; no numeric axis ticks or values are printed. Series NVIDIA (green bar, tall, about 27.6% of slide height) and CPU (blue bar, short, horizontal, about 12.3% of slide width) drawn as bars with no printed values; legend box at right with green square 'NVIDIA' and blue square 'CPU'. Right panel: a line chart titled 'GPT-MoE-1.8T energy per token' with year labels 2014 at the left and 2024 at the right on a horizontal grey baseline; green line with circular markers at Kepler 42000 Joules/Token, Pascal 17640 J/Token, Volta 1200 J/Token, Ampere 150 J/Token, Hopper 10 J/Token, Blackwell 0.4 J/Token, falling steeply from left to right and flattening near the baseline. No highlighted points beyond the green markers. NVIDIA logo bottom right in black and green.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White slide background with two light grey #F7F7F7 panels, each with a thin light grey #BFBFBF border and square corners; no shadows or gradients. Title in NVIDIASans Bold black #000000 at about 4.4% of slide height; subtitle in NVIDIASans Regular green #76B900 at about 3.0%; body text in NVIDIASans Regular black #000000 at about 2.0% with bold green #76B900 emphasis phrases; chart labels in NVIDIASans Regular black at about 1.4% to 1.7%. Accent green #76B900, bars blue #0070C0 and green #76B900, axes mid grey #808080 hairlines.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.
- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

AI Scaling Laws Drive Exponential Demand for Compute
New OpenAI o1 Long “Thinking Time” Creates a New Way to Scale
Training compute scales exponentially with larger models, multi-modality, reinforcement learning, and synthetic data generation
Training Compute
Inference compute scales exponentially with larger models, multi-modality, large context, low latency, and now long “thinking time”
Inference Compute
o1
12

**Layout**

Single centred title block at the top with a two-line subtitle beneath it, both centred on the slide. A large light grey framed panel fills the middle of the slide, with a diagram of two rising arrows on axes drawn inside it; the descriptive text sits to the right of each arrow. The NVIDIA logo is at bottom right, with a thin green bar at the far right edge and the page number just left of it.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a two-axis chart drawn with thin lines, with no printed numeric scale, ticks or units. The origin is at the lower left of the panel. The vertical axis runs from the origin up to about 35 percent of slide height and the horizontal axis runs right from the origin for about 35 percent of slide width. Dark green (#008564) arrow, about 4 px stroke, runs from the origin diagonally up and right to an arrowhead, labelled Training Compute (bold, placed above-left of the arrow) and described by the text Training compute scales exponentially with larger models, multi-modality, reinforcement learning, and synthetic data generation. Grey line (about #888888, about 3 px) runs from the origin to a green circle labelled o1 (white text), then a light green arrow (#92D050, about 4 px) continues from the circle up and right to an arrowhead; this arrow is labelled Inference Compute (bold, placed above-left of the arrow) and described by the text Inference compute scales exponentially with larger models, multi-modality, large context, low latency, and now long thinking time. The o1 circle is solid #76B900, about 2.7 percent of slide width in diameter, sitting on the grey and light green lines where they meet. No legend, no highlighted points beyond the o1 circle, no photographs. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with eye symbol in black, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in black NVIDIASans Bold, sans-serif, about 4.4 percent of slide height (cap height about 3.1 percent). Subtitle in NVIDIA green #76B900, NVIDIASans Regular, about 3.0 percent of slide height, with OpenAI o1 in italic. Diagram text in black NVIDIASans Regular at about 2.0 percent of slide height, with the category labels in bold. Panel is flat #F7F7F7 with a thin light grey border (about #C8C8C8, 1 px). Lines are thin grey and dark green, with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Green accent: #76B900 bar at right edge and o1 circle.

**Layout**

Three equal columns of light grey boxed panels sit side by side below a centred title and subtitle, with the title block at the top centre and the panels spanning roughly x 4.5% to 95.5% and y 23.6% to 82.0%. Each panel has a centred heading at its top, with a graphic in its lower area; a small NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right with the page number 13 to its left and a short green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Three graphics. (1) Left panel: a line chart drawn as a curve with no printed numeric axis labels. The vertical axis is a thin grey line at about x 9.3% from y 45.7% to 76.4%; the horizontal axis is a thin grey line at about y 76.4% from x 9.3% to 29.1%. A green (#76B900) curve, about 3 pt wide, starts at the origin near x 10.7%, y 76.4%, rises gently then steepens into an exponential-looking climb, and ends in a solid green arrowhead pointing up and to the right at about x 27.5% to 28.5%, y 45.5% to 48.5%. Nine labelled terms are centred over the curve in dark text: Flash Attention, KVCache PageAttention, Distillation, Pruning & Quantization, Neural Architecture Search, Disaggregated Serving, Speculative Decoding, Multi-GPU, Multi-Node. (2) Centre panel: a green (#76B900) spiral illustration, about 3 pt stroke, made of stacked ellipse loops that shrink downward like a tornado or coil, starting at the bottom near x 48%, y 86% and widening to the top right, ending in a solid green arrowhead pointing up-left at about x 52.2% to 54.7%, y 43.2% to 47.3%. No labels. (3) Right panel: a stylised GPU chip diagram. A central green (#76B900) square chip body at x 74.3% to 87.7%, y 49.3% to 73.2%, with thin orange (#F58B00-like) grid lines drawn across its edge and a lighter inner green rectangle at x 75.6% to 86.7%, y 52.6% to 69.5%. Around it are green rectangular pin blocks: a top row and a bottom row of about 14 small squares each (top at y 45.7% to 48.9%, bottom at y 73.6% to 76.8%, both spanning x 74.5% to 87.6%), and left and right columns of about 10 squares each (left at x 72.4% to 74.2%, right at x 87.9% to 89.8%, both spanning y 49.6% to 73.1%). White text on the inner rectangle reads GB200 NVL72 / NVLink Switches / 130 TB/s All-to-All BW, then a gap, then One Giant Blackwell / 1.44 EF FP4 / 576TB/s HBM3e. The NVIDIA logo is small, black with a green mark. No photographs; all graphics are flat with no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide. Clean white slide with flat light grey panels. Title is bold black NVIDIA Sans (close to a humanist grotesque such as Helvetica Bold) at about 4.4% of slide height, centred. Subtitle is regular NVIDIA green #76B900 at about 3.0% of slide height, centred, with curly quotes. Panel headings and body labels are regular NVIDIA Sans in black #000000 at about 2.0% of slide height, centred, with the terms list at about 1.5%. Accent green #76B900 is used for the subtitle, the curve, the spiral, the chip and the right-edge bar. Panels are #F7F7F7 with a thin grey border of about 0.75 pt (#BFBFBF), square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Arrow glyphs ➔ are set in Segoe UI Symbol in black. Chip grid lines are thin orange (#F58B00) on the chip edges.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

NVIDIA AI Infrastructure Roadmap at One Year Rhythm
NVIDIA’s newest architecture – Blackwell – is an AI infrastructure platform and integrates seven chips,
each contributing to performance at data center-scale.
Compute demand for AI is scaling exponentially. Model size, multi-modality, synthetic data generation and
reinforcement learning is scaling computing capacity by ~4x per year. And new inference-time technologies like
multi-modality, Chain-of-Thought, reasoning, and agents have introduced an exponential scaling law to inference.
NVIDIA has the scale and ability to update the entire AI infrastructure on a One Year Rhythm as we can engineer
and optimize across the full stack and the entire infrastructure.
The cadence and breadth of our innovation brings more performant AI infrastructure to the market each year,
delivering exceptional TCO, energy efficiency, and ROI to our customers.

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column on a white field, starting at about 3.9% from the left edge. The title sits at about 29% from the top with the body paragraphs stacked beneath it; the upper third of the slide is empty. A full-height green vertical bar runs along the left edge in the upper part of the slide, and a small NVIDIA logo sits at the bottom right with a short green bar beside it. No footer, slide number or other text.

**Visuals**

None apart from the NVIDIA logo at bottom right, which is a green-and-black eye-shaped mark followed by the black wordmark NVIDIA with a small trademark dot; it is not a photograph, chart or diagram.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold black #000000 sans-serif (NVIDIASans Bold, close to a humanist grotesque), at about 4.4% of slide height, single line. Body in regular black #000000 sans-serif (NVIDIASans Regular), about 2.2% of slide height, line spacing about 3.2% of slide height, with one blank line between paragraphs. The only bold phrase in the body is TCO, energy efficiency, and ROI. Accent colour is NVIDIA green #76B900, used for the left vertical bar and the small bar at bottom right. No shapes with rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.
- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

One Year Rhythm | Supercluster Scale | Full-Stack | CUDA Everywhere / “Supercharge AI Scaling Law” / X-Factors / Hopper / Blackwell / Blackwell-Ultra / Rubin / X-Factors / X-Factors / Hopper GPU / 6S HBM3 / Hopper+ GPU / 6S HBM3e / Grace CPU / NVLink Switch / 900 GB/sec / CX7 / SuperNIC / BF3 / SuperNIC / Quantum-X400 / Infiniband Switch / GB200 NVL72 / HGX Blackwell / NVLINK Switch / GB200 Superchip / Compute Node / Quantum X800 Switch / ConnectX-8 SuperNIC / Spectrum X800 Switch / BlueField-3 SuperNIC / Blackwell Ultra GPU / 288GB HBM3e / More AI FLOPS / CX8 / SuperNIC / Spectrum Ultra X800 / Ethernet Switch 512-Radix / Rubin GPU / 8S HBM4 / Vera CPU / NVLink 6 Switch / 3600 GB/sec / CX9 SuperNIC / 1600 Gb/sec / X1600 / IB/Ethernet Switch / Rubin GPU / 12S HBM4 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 / 2027

**Layout**

Full-width centred title and green subtitle sit in the top band, above a wide black infographic panel that spans the slide with equal side margins of about 4.5%. Inside the panel, four labelled columns (Hopper, Blackwell, Blackwell-Ultra, Rubin) sit in rounded outlined boxes along a horizontal timeline arrow with years 2022 to 2027 below; the NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a green bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Infographic-style product roadmap on a black panel. Hopper column (2022-2023): small gold-framed chip icons for Hopper GPU 6S HBM3, Hopper+ GPU 6S HBM3e, Grace CPU, NVLink Switch 900 GB/sec, CX7 SuperNIC, BF3 SuperNIC and Quantum-X400 Infiniband Switch, arranged in two loose columns. Blackwell column (2024): a large photographic render of the GB200 NVL72 rack, dark grey racks with blue and gold cable runs, on a dark studio floor with soft spotlight, framed inside the box; below it are small product photos labelled HGX Blackwell, NVLINK Switch, GB200 Superchip Compute Node, Quantum X800 Switch with ConnectX-8 SuperNIC, and Spectrum X800 Switch with BlueField-3 SuperNIC. Blackwell-Ultra column (2025): a vertical strip with a Blackwell Ultra GPU chip image, a CX8 SuperNIC chip and a Spectrum Ultra X800 Ethernet Switch 512-Radix chip, all gold-tinted icons on black. Rubin column (2026-2027): gold-tinted chip icons for Rubin GPU 8S HBM4, Vera CPU, NVLink 6 Switch, CX9 SuperNIC, X1600 IB/Ethernet Switch on the 2026 side, and three further faint chip icons plus Rubin GPU 12S HBM4 on the 2027 side. Three gold arrows labelled X-Factors point upward above the Blackwell, Blackwell-Ultra and Rubin columns at increasing heights. A thin white horizontal timeline arrow runs left to right beneath the columns with year labels. Lighting on the chips is a soft gold rim glow; the NVIDIA logo is the only brand mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF slide background, with a black #000000 panel covering most of the slide. Clean white slide with a black infographic panel. Title in heavy black sans-serif (NVIDIA Sans Bold), approximately 4.4% of slide height, colour #000000. Subtitle in regular green sans-serif, about 3.0% of slide height, colour #76B900. Column headers and X-Factors labels in medium gold-khaki sans-serif, about 1.5%, colour #AC995F. Product labels in small white regular sans-serif, about 1.0%, colour #FFFFFF. Year labels white regular sans-serif, about 1.5%. Column outlines are thin khaki-gold 1px rounded rectangles (about 1% corner radius) with dark grey interior tint, roughly #111111. Timeline arrow is a thin white line. Chip icons have a gold rim glow. No shadows or gradients on the slide itself; the photo has a soft vignette.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Side-by-side cluster comparisons with GPU count and power make the cost reduction across generations concrete in one glance.
- Review: Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.
- What would change the score: Redraw slides 16 and 26 so the visuals show the proportion and the trend directly, for example scaled icons or a bar for the power drop, and separate H1 FY25 from full-year bars with a simpler margin treatment.

**Text**

One Year Rhythm Drives Annual Cost and Energy Reduction / Significant reduction in TCO with each generation / Hopper | 8,000 GPUs | 15MW / Blackwell GB200 NVL72 | 2,000 GPUs | 4MW / 4X / Reduction in Power / LLM Training Workload: GPT-MoE-1.8T | Train in 90 days | H100 vs GB200 NVL72

**Layout**

Two equal side-by-side panels sit in the middle band of the slide below a centred two-line header, with a centred italic footer line near the bottom and the NVIDIA logo at bottom right. The left panel spans x 4.5 to 49.2 and the right panel x 50.8 to 95.5, with equal gutters of about 1.6% and side margins of about 4.5%. The title is centred at the top, with no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Left panel: five identical server-rack icons in a single row, each a tall rectangle outlined in NVIDIA green (#76B900) with six horizontal shelf dividers and three short dash marks per shelf, drawn as simple line icons with no fill; they are evenly spaced, bottom-aligned on one baseline at about y 49.8 to 69.6, and represent the Hopper configuration. Right panel: one server-rack icon of the same style at the left-centre (x 61.0, y 49.8, w 11.1, h 19.8), with to its right a small black downward arrow (a thin vertical line with an arrowhead, about x 73.8, y 55.8, h 4.5) followed by the large bold figure '4X' and the caption 'Reduction in Power' centred beneath it. No chart, photograph or table. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark in black with the green eye symbol at bottom right (x 93.1, y 94.8, w 5.5, h 3.4).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles are black (#000000) NVIDIASans Bold sans-serif in a large heavy weight; the subtitle is a light-green (#92D050) NVIDIASans Regular sans-serif. Panel labels and captions are black NVIDIASans Medium/Regular sans-serif. Panels are light grey (#F7F7F7) rectangles with a hairline grey border (about #BFBFBF, 0.5 pt), square corners, no shadow. Server icons use NVIDIA green (#76B900) strokes of about 0.5% of slide width. A thin vertical green (#76B900) bar sits at the bottom-right edge. Title about 4.4% of slide height, subtitle 3.0%, panel labels 2.5%, 4X 5.9%, body caption 2.0%, footer 1.5%.

The bottom right holds the page number 17, the NVIDIA logo and a short vertical green bar along the right edge; no footer text, no left margin elements.

**Visuals**

1) Photograph of an NVLink Switch tray, horizontal, shown open from the front-left at a slight angle: a silver/grey chassis with exposed black circuit board, blue and white cabling, copper-coloured heatsinks and black coolant hoses curving off the right side; lit evenly by studio light, full-colour, boxed with no border, no overlay, with a soft fading shadow at the bottom edge. Label 'NVLink Switch' sits above it. 2) Black arrow (thin line with small arrowhead pointing right) running from the right edge of the switch photo to the left edge of the rack. 3) Two dark rack images side by side, both tall portrait: the left is a front view of a black server rack with a small switch unit near the top and rows of gold-edged compute trays stacked down the middle, with a translucent white rectangle outlined in green #76B900 (about 0.2% stroke) highlighting one unit at about 52% height; the right is a rear view of the same rack, black, with a central vertical panel of grey cabling/ribbons between two gold-edged frames and vent grilles at top and bottom; both racks have feet at the base. Crisp flat vector, no shadow, no gradient.

**Design**

Title cap height about 4.4% of slide height (font size 4.44% of slide height); body and label text about 1.5% to 1.7% of slide height. ### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

NVIDIA Addresses The Entire AI Market
NVIDIA’s accelerated computing platform encompasses a complete computing stack
and infrastructure required for customers to build and run AI at scale.
CUDA-X libraries and microservices leverage the programmability of CUDA to enable thousands
of applications across a wide range of use cases to run on NVIDIA infrastructure, including AI
training and inference, data processing, robotics, drug discovery, and consumer internet services.
NVIDIA has a rich ecosystem of partners who have integrated NVIDIA platform technologies and
joined our go-to-market.  Each industry and market segment may include different domain-
specific software stacks, ODMs, OEMs, CSPs, 3rd party software platforms, system integrators,
and system integrations/solution development partners.
The completeness of the NVIDIA platform for each industry and workload lets us address every
industry from healthcare, manufacturing, and transportation to retail, CSPs, and
telecommunications.

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column starting at about 3.9% from the left edge, with the title at about 29% of the slide height and the body paragraphs stacked beneath it. The right half of the slide is empty; the NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right with a short green bar at the far right edge, and a green bar runs down the left edge at top.

**Visuals**

Two non-text elements. (1) A solid green vertical bar at the top-left edge, x 0 to 1.5%, y 0 to 32.9%, filled #76B900, flush with the left edge of the slide and ending at the title's vertical level. (2) The NVIDIA logo at bottom right, x 93.1%, y 94.8%, w 5.5%, h 3.4%: the black NVIDIA eye mark followed by the black NVIDIA wordmark with a small trademark dot, on a white ground. A short solid green bar sits at the bottom-right corner, about x 99.4%, y 95%, w 0.6%, h 4.2%, filled #76B900. No charts, photographs or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no gradients, shadows or textures. Title in NVIDIASans Bold, black #000000, about 4.4% of slide height, sentence case with the words capitalised as printed. Body in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 2.2% of slide height with generous line spacing (about 3.2% of height per line) and blank lines between paragraphs. Bold runs in NVIDIASans Bold for 'NVIDIA’s accelerated computing platform' and 'CUDA-X'. Accent colour NVIDIA green #76B900 used only for the two bars. Lines and shapes are square-cornered with no outlines.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Move the disclaimer to the appendix or shrink it to a short notice, and rewrite the text-heavy slides 5, 7, 10, 14, 18 and 20 into a few large statements with a visual, and enlarge the tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.

**Text**

AI Driving Investment Cycle / and Significant Returns
AI is accelerating scientific discovery, creativity, / and productivity across industries
AI Agents, or copilots and AI teammates, automate tasks at superhuman / speed, increase employee productivity and accelerate businesses. Agents / can generate documents, review contracts, help code and debug software, / operationalize marketing campaigns, and review medical images
Social media, search and e-commerce apps are using deep recommenders / to offer more relevant content and generative AI to hyper-customize ads to / their customers, increasing engagement and monetization
Creators can generate stunning, photorealistic images or 3D objects with a / single text prompt—compressing workflows that take days or weeks into / minutes in industries from advertising to game development
Call center agents augmented with AI chatbots can scale to deliver / customer care during peak times, run in-the-loop with customer support / to provide better service, and capture every interaction to build institutional / knowledge in high turnover industries
Drug discovery is seeing order-of-magnitude workflow acceleration using / generative AI for candidate drug generation and target selection
Manufacturing is using generative AI to generate ideas to inspire designers, / accelerate the planning and layout of factories and warehouses; and for / self-learning robots that can handle diverse tasks in changing work / environments
Climate Tech scientists are using AI to discover new materials for better / batteries, emulate complex physics to predict long-range weather, estimate / carbon-capture sites, optimize wind farm output
AI Agents & Copilots / Over 1B / knowledge workers
Search & Social Media / $700B in / digital advertising annually
AI Content Creation / 50M creators / globally
Legal Services, / Education / 1M legal professionals in the US / 9M educators in the US
AI Software / Development / 30M software developers / globally
Financial / Services / 678B annual / credit card transactions
Customer Service / 15M call center agents / globally
Drug Discovery / 10¹⁸ molecules in chemical space / 40 exabytes of genome data
Manufacturing / $50T / of Heavy Industry
Source: Goldman Sachs, Cowen, Statista, Capital One, Wall Street Journal, Resource Watch, NVIDIA internal analysis

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: the left column (about 0 to 47 percent of width) holds the title, green subtitle, a horizontal rule and seven bold-lead body paragraphs, left-aligned from a margin at about 7.3 percent. The right column (about 55 to 100 percent) is a light grey panel holding a 3 by 3 grid of green tiles, each with a white line icon and centred green label and black caption beneath. The source line sits at the bottom left beside the NVIDIA logo; there is no page number.

**Visuals**

Nine icon tiles in a 3 by 3 grid, each a solid #76B900 square with a white outline icon. Row 1: AI Agents and Copilots (outline of a person bust with tie above a rectangular audio waveform box); Search and Social Media (two speech-bubble outlines with text lines, a play-button bubble and a picture bubble linked by circular arrows); AI Content Creation (monitor showing a paint palette beside a tower PC). Row 2: Legal Services, Education (outline gavel with a block); AI Software Development (stacked chip and server outlines with circuit nodes); Financial Services (rising line over bar chart with a dollar coin). Row 3: Customer Service (headset-wearing agent bust); Drug Discovery (outline of a stacked molecular capsule shape); Manufacturing (outline factory with smokestacks and windowed hall). Icons are line art, about 1 pt white strokes, centred in each tile. The NVIDIA logo is a small black wordmark with green eye mark. A thin green bar sits at the bottom-left edge.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF across the left half, with the right panel in light grey (#F2F2F2 at 61 percent opacity). White left half and a light grey right panel (#F2F2F2 at 61 percent opacity, reading as about #F8F8F8). Title and body text black #000000; subtitle and tile labels NVIDIA green #76B900; source line grey #8C8C8C. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans (a geometric grotesque sans-serif): Bold for the title and paragraph leads, Regular for body and captions, Medium for tile labels, Italic for the source. Title cap height about 4.4 percent of slide height (font size 4.44 percent), subtitle 2.96 percent, body 1.98 percent, tile labels 1.85 percent, captions 1.36 percent, source 1.23 percent. Horizontal rule is a thin mid-grey line. Tiles are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Enterprise AI Wave is Here
Co-Pilots, AI Teammates, and Agents are the New Digital Workforce to Revolution Enterprise Productivity
Mayfield People First.
Our Founders / Our Team / Our Perspectives / Our Product
Our AI Teammates Family
Data Engineer / ITOps Engineer / Sales Engineer / Security Teams / Support Engineer / Sales Development Rep / Healthcare Professional / Finance Manager / Security Analyst / Chip Engineer
REVEFI / NEUBIRD / Docket / Balbix / DevRev / Outreach / Qventus / Scma4.ai / STEALTH / STEALTH
This is Mayfield's webpage of AI companies in their portfolio. The illustration and description provide a framework of enterprise AI – agents are a new digital workforce that will collaborate and accelerate the work of employees.
Specialized AI co-pilots, teammates, or agents will collaborate with employees, be given access to necessary data, use platform tools like SAP, Snowflake, or ServiceNow, and even collaborate with other AI agents.
In time, the world's companies will hire billions of AI agents in its workforce.
NVIDIA AI Enterprise offers the IT and services ecosystem, NVIDIA-optimized NeMo agent lifecycle libraries, NIM pre-trained models, and DGX Cloud infrastructure to build and deploy enterprise AI.
Enterprises license NVIDIA AI Enterprise at $4500 per-GPU per-Year to run NIM and NeMo libraries.
NVIDIA

**Layout**

Centred title block at the top with a bold black headline and a green subtitle beneath it, both centred on the slide. Below, a two-column arrangement: a boxed webpage screenshot occupies the left column from about 4.5% to 58% of the width, and a left-aligned text column of six paragraphs sits in the right column starting at about 63% of the width. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a thin green bar at the far right edge; no slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

One illustration: a screenshot of Mayfield's webpage 'Our AI Teammates Family', shown inside a thin light-grey bbordered box (about 2617 by 1725 pixels, not cropped). Top strip is a light grey (#F5F5F5) navigation bar with the Mayfield 'People First.' script logo at left, a boxed nav group reading 'Our Founders', 'Our Team', 'Our Perspectives', 'Our Product' with a search magnifier icon at centre-right. Below, a white panel with a serif heading 'Our AI Teammates Family' centred. Ten stylised 3D cartoon teammate avatars (head and shoulders in dark grey-black suits with glowing cyan chest cores) each surrounded by a green (#4CAF50 to #2E7D32 gradient) partial ring arc open on one side, arranged in three rows: row 1 has four (red-haired woman, black-haired man, red-haired man, dark-haired woman), row 2 has four (dark-haired woman, blond man, blond woman, dark-haired man), row 3 has two (brown-haired man, dark-haired woman). Under each avatar is a company logo and a role label: REVEFI (Data Engineer); NEUBIRD (ITOps Engineer); Docket (Sales Engineer); Balbix (Security Teams); DevRev (Support Engineer); Outreach (Sales Development Rep); Qventus (Healthcare Professional); Scma4.ai (Finance Manager); STEALTH in green capitals (Security Analyst); STEALTH in green capitals (Chip Engineer). Lighting is flat and even on the white panel; dominant colours are white, grey, green and cyan.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a flow diagram occupies the left half (about x 11 to 48 percent) and a green boxed ecosystem panel occupies the right (about x 56.5 to 89.5 percent). The two-line bold title is centred across the top (y 2.5 to 11.6 percent) and is not left-aligned; the right panel heading sits above the panel. A small NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about x 93 percent, y 95 percent, with a thin green accent bar at the right edge.

**Visuals**

Left: a flow diagram. A user icon (blue-outlined circle containing a blue person silhouette, about x 27.9 y 20.4 w 3.5 h 6.2 percent) sits above the word User. Two thin black arrows run vertically between User and the AI Agent: one pointing down and one pointing up. The AI Agent is a light grey (#E6E6E6) circle with a thin green outline containing a green robot-head glyph, a green atom-like icon and a green audio-waveform bar; it overlaps the top edge of a large rounded rectangle. Inside the rounded rectangle: a Database icon (stacked orange cylinders with a small orange database-settings badge) with a small green-pixelated cube at its upper right; a black elbow connector runs from the database and vector icons on the left to the NVIDIA NIM hexagonal cube, with arrowheads pointing at the database (top) and at the vector database (bottom) and an arrow pointing right into NIM. The NVIDIA NIM icon is a green-and-grey isometric cube with green nodes; a black arrow runs right from NIM to an Action icon (a square with a thin tan border containing an orange gear cluster). NVIDIA NeMo Retriever Vector Database: an orange-outlined square with an orange node-graph glyph, plus a small green-and-grey isometric cube. Below the rounded rectangle, a Data Flywheel icon (a grey-green faceted cube) sits at the centre of four curved green arrows forming a circular cycle; the lower arrow loops into the NVIDIA NeMo hexagon icon (green outline hexagon with a green node-tree glyph, lime green #76B900 strokes). Right: a green (#76B900) rectangle with four dark olive (#598B00) full-width header bars, each with white centred text, and white vendor logotypes beneath each header: accenture (wordmark with a chevron above the e), Deloitte. (bold wordmark), and an ellipsis; amdocs (a-shaped mark plus wordmark), cadence (wordmark with macron accents), SAP (white box with stylised wordmark), servicenow (wordmark), SYNOPSYS (wordmark), and an ellipsis; CLOUDERA (spaced capitals), Dropbox (diamond mark plus wordmark), NetApp (mark plus wordmark), ORACLE (spaced capitals), VAST (inverted triangle mark plus spaced capitals), and an ellipsis; aws (wordmark with smile arrow), DELL Technologies (wordmark), Google Cloud (cloud mark plus wordmark), Hewlett Packard Enterprise (rectangular mark plus two-line wordmark), Microsoft Azure (four-square Windows mark plus two-line wordmark), ORACLE CLOUD Infrastructure (stacked wordmark), and an ellipsis. All logos are white. The NVIDIA logo is a dark green-grey wordmark with eye mark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF background, no gradient, photograph or pattern. Clean, flat corporate style. Title is bold black sans-serif, about 4.4 percent of slide height per line (roughly cap height 3.1 percent). Panel heading NVIDIA AI Enterprise Ecosystem is medium-weight black sans-serif at 2.5 percent. Diagram labels are regular black sans-serif at about 1.5 to 2.0 percent. Panel header labels are medium-weight white sans-serif at about 2.0 percent. Colours: title black #000000, body text black #000000, panel white #FFFFFF text, accent NVIDIA green #76B900, header bars #598B00, diagram outline green #76B900, AI Agent circle fill #E6E6E6, database orange #D9622B, Action frame tan #C8A46E, bottom-right accent bar #76B900. Typefaces: NVIDIA Sans family (close to a humanist grotesque sans-serif). Shapes: the green panel and header bars have square corners; the diagram container has rounded corners of about 1 percent of slide width and a 1 pt-equivalent green stroke; arrows are thin black lines with small filled arrowheads. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 24

**Text**

NVIDIA Omniverse and AI Revolutionizing Manufacturing & Robotics
OMNIVERSE
AI
ROBOT
The next AI wave is physical AI - models that can perceive, understand, and interact with the physical world. Physical AI will embody robotic systems – from autonomous vehicles to industrial robots and humanoids, to warehouses and factories.
Three computers and software stacks are required to build physical AI:
NVIDIA AI on DGX to train the AI model, NVIDIA Omniverse on OVX to teach, test, and validate the AI model's skills, and NVIDIA AGX to run the AI software on the robot.
Enterprises license NVIDIA Omniverse at $4500 per-GPU per-Year.
100M Cars
Billions in Future
10M Factories
200K Warehouses
24

**Layout**

Centered bold title sits in the top band across most of the width. The left column (about 4% to 56% of width) holds a circular-orbit diagram above three paragraphs of body text. The right side holds a 2x2 grid of circular photographs, each with a caption centred beneath it. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right with a page number beside a short green bar at the far right edge. Margins are roughly 4 to 6% on the left.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a thin grey ellipse outline with three nodes on it. At the top centre, a circular photo labelled OMNIVERSE shows a kitchen scene with a robotic arm and a cup, with a faint blue-white glow edge. At the lower left, a beige NVIDIA DGX computer chassis (rectangular box with a black front panel and green NVIDIA logo, with a stacked GPU top) is labelled AI. At the lower right, a silver-white humanoid robot standing on a black NVIDIA Jetson-style AGX module is labelled ROBOT; the robot has a purple glowing chest light. Photo 1 (top right, 100M Cars): a red electric SUV in three-quarter front view on a black background, lit from above, full circle crop. Photo 2 (top far right, Billions in Future): a silver humanoid robot in a grey kitchen reaching to a shelf with a cookware and a pot on a marble counter, warm soft lighting, full circle crop. Photo 3 (bottom left, 10M Factories): white industrial robot arms with green joints over a conveyor and tray system in a bright white factory, a blue FI logo on a panel at lower left, full circle crop. Photo 4 (bottom right, 200K Warehouses): a digital-twin render of a warehouse with yellow racks, blue and yellow bounding boxes around pallets and forklift-like vehicles on a grey floor, full circle crop. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark in black with eye mark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background.

### Slide 25

**Text**

Sovereign AI / Nations produce AI using their own data, infrastructure, workforce, and business networks / Sovereign AI / Nations are awakening to the imperative / to produce artificial intelligence using / their own infrastructure, data, / workforces and business networks. / Nations are building domestic computing / capacity. / Some governments operate sovereign AI / clouds in collaboration with state-owned / telecommunications providers or utilities. / Other governments partner with local / cloud providers to deliver a shared AI / computing infrastructure for public- and / private-sector use. / NVIDIA’s ability to help build AI / infrastructure with our end-to-end / compute-to-networking technologies, / full-stack software, AI expertise, and rich / ecosystem of partners and customers / allows sovereign AI and regional cloud / providers to jumpstart their countries’ / AI ambitions. / France / Scaleway / Switzerland / Swisscom Group / Spain / Barcelona / Supercomputing / Center / Ecuador / Telconet / Vietnam / FPT Smart Cloud / Japan / National Institute of / Advanced Industrial Science / and Technology (AIST) / Singapore / Singapore / Telecommunications Limited / (Singtel) / Location of NVIDIA Sovereign AI partners / 25

**Layout**

Centred title and green subtitle sit across the top of the slide with the title on one centred axis. A light grey text panel occupies the left column from about 2.4% to 26.9% of the width and from 23.3% to 86.2% of the height, with its heading and body text left-aligned inside it. The world map fills the right and centre of the slide behind the panel, with callout boxes placed over it and linked to green markers by leader lines. A legend sits bottom-left, the NVIDIA logo bottom-right and the page number bottom-right beside a green vertical bar at the right edge.

**Visuals**

A full-width grey world map illustration (about 88% of slide width, from 6.0% to 94.0% horizontally and 18.5% to 95.7% vertically) with landmasses in light grey #D3D3D3 on a white ocean, showing faint national borders, with no labels. Twenty-seven solid green circular markers (#76B900 fill with a dark green #4A7300 outline) sit on partner locations: twelve in Western and Northern Europe, three in the Americas (one in North America, one in Ecuador, one in Brazil), one in southern Africa, three in the Middle East, one in India, two in mainland Southeast Asia, two in Singapore area, and two in East Asia (Korea and Japan) plus one in southern China. Five white callout boxes with thin grey borders show France, Switzerland, Spain, Japan, Vietnam, Ecuador and Singapore, each linked to a marker by a thin dark grey leader line. A legend marker (green circle) with italic caption sits bottom-left. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Paired revenue and operating income figures with margin percentages tell the growth story in a clean, readable table format.
- Review: Several pages have real craft, such as the networking curve on slide 9 and the bar charts on slide 26.
- Review: Slide 16's comparison is weak, since five rack icons against one icon do not match the 8,000 versus 2,000 GPU claim visually, and slide 26 uses overlaid H1 bars and a margin line that are hard to read, with tiny labels on slides 11, 12 and 15.
- What would change the score: Redraw slides 16 and 26 so the visuals show the proportion and the trend directly, for example scaled icons or a bar for the power drop, and separate H1 FY25 from full-year bars with a simpler margin treatment.

**Text**

Driving Strong and Profitable Growth / Revenue ($M) / $60,922 / $56,084 / $26,914 / $26,974 / $12,690 / $16,675 / $10,918 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / FY24 / H1 FY25 / Operating Income ($M) / Operating Margin % / $37,997 / $37,134 / 68% / 61% / $6,803 / $9,040 / 47% / 34% / $3,735 / 34% / 41% / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / FY24 / H1 FY25 / Non-GAAP / Fiscal year ends in January. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures. Operating margins rounded to the nearest percent.

**Layout**

Title is centred horizontally at the top of the slide. Two equal-width light grey panels sit side by side in the middle band, the left panel for Revenue and the right panel for Operating Income with Operating Margin, each with a green heading at its top-left and a bar chart with fiscal-year category labels along its base. A small italic footnote sits below the right panel, left-aligned to its edge. The NVIDIA logo sits at the bottom right with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge; no slide number is shown.

**Visuals**

Two bar charts on light grey panels. Left chart: Revenue in $M as green columns for FY20 to H1 FY25, with no visible value axis, labelled FY20 $10,918, FY21 $16,675, FY22 $26,914, FY23 $26,974, FY24 $60,922 and H1 FY25 $56,084. FY24 is a two-tone column (bright green top, dark green bottom segment) and H1 FY25 is a dark green column; a pale green translucent wedge with a thin black arrow rises from the FY24 dark segment to the top of the H1 FY25 column. Right chart: Operating Income in $M as green columns with operating margin percentages printed on or above them, FY20 $3,735 34%, FY21 $6,803 41%, FY22 $12,690 47%, FY23 $9,040 34%, FY24 $37,134 61%, H1 FY25 $37,997 68%. A thin black line connects the margin points (FY20 34%, FY21 41%, FY22 47%, FY23 34%, FY24 61%, H1 FY25 68%) across the chart, rising sharply from FY23 to FY24 and continuing to H1 FY25. FY24 is two-tone (bright green top, dark green bottom) and H1 FY25 is dark green with a pale green translucent wedge and thin black arrow from FY24 to the H1 FY25 top. No value axis or legend is printed; the lines and arrows are highlighting the trend. The NVIDIA wordmark logo is at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with a sans-serif NVIDIA corporate typeface. Title is black #000000 in bold sans-serif at about 4.4 percent of slide height. Panel headings are bold NVIDIA green #76B900 at about 2.5 percent. Chart labels and category labels are regular black #000000 at about 2.0 percent. The footnote is italic grey #8C8C8C at about 1.2 percent. Panels are flat #F7F7F7 with square corners and no shadow. Bars use #76B900 with dark green #598B00 accents and a pale green #D2E5AA translucent wedge. Connector lines are thin black #000000 strokes of about 1 px width with no fill.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Strong Cash Flow Generation / Free Cash Flow (Non-GAAP) / $28.4B / $26.9B / $8.0B / $4.3B / $4.7B / $3.8B / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / FY24 / H1 FY25 / 1 / Capital Allocation / Share Repurchase / • Utilized $14.9B of cash for repurchases in H1 FY25 / • Additional $50B in stock repurchase authorization, adding to $7.5B which remained as of the end of Q2 / Dividend / • $344M in H1 FY25 / • Dividend increased by 150% in Q2 FY25 / • Plan to Maintain 1 / Strategic Investments / • Growing Our Talent / • Platform Reach & Ecosystem / Fiscal year ends in January. Refer to Appendix for reconciliation of Non-GAAP measures. / 1 Subject to continuing determination by our Board of Directors.

**Layout**

Centred bold title across the top at about 7 percent from the top. Below it two equal-height light grey panels sit side by side with a gap of about 6 percent: the left panel holds a vertical bar chart with its heading at top left, the right panel holds bulleted capital allocation text with headings at top left. Panel left edge at 4.5 percent and right edge at 95.5 percent, panels span 23.6 to 82.0 percent of slide height. Footnotes sit left-aligned under each panel at about 83 percent of height. NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about 95 percent height, with a thin green vertical bar at the far right edge. No slide number is printed.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart, titled Free Cash Flow (Non-GAAP), in the left panel. Y axis has no printed scale or units; bars are labelled with values. X axis categories left to right: FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, FY24, H1 FY25. Series values printed above bars: FY20 $4.3B, FY21 $4.7B, FY22 $8.0B, FY23 $3.8B, FY24 $26.9B, H1 FY25 $28.4B. FY20 to FY23 bars are solid light green #76B900. FY24 bar is split: upper segment light green #76B900 from about 35 to 61 percent of slide height, lower segment dark green #598B00 from about 61 to 73 percent. H1 FY25 bar is dark green #598B00 and is the highlighted bar. A pale green #D2E5A8 translucent triangle-shaped connector runs from the top of the FY24 dark segment (upper left) up to the top of the H1 FY25 bar (upper right), with a thin black diagonal line along its top edge ending in a small arrowhead at the H1 FY25 top. Baseline is a thin grey #8C8C8C horizontal axis line across the bottom of the bars. No legend. Bar heights are proportional to the printed values. Right panel contains no graphics other than the green bullet dots. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with eye symbol in black #000000 at bottom right. No photographs or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with flat light grey panels. Title in NVIDIASans Bold black #000000, about 4.4 percent of slide height for the cap-height line. Section headings in NVIDIASans Bold green #76B900, about 2.5 percent of height. Sub-headings in NVIDIASans Bold black #000000, about 2.2 percent. ### Slide 28

**Text**

Our Market Platforms at a Glance / Data Center / 78% of FY24 Revenue / FY24 Revenue $47.5B / 5-YR CAGR 75% / DGX/HGX/MGX/IGX systems / GPU | CPU | DPU | Networking / NVIDIA AI software / Gaming / 17% of FY24 Revenue / FY24 Revenue $10.4B / 5-YR CAGR 11% / GeForce GPUs for PC gaming / GeForce NOW cloud gaming / Professional Visualization / 3% of FY24 Revenue / FY24 Revenue $1.6B / 5-YR CAGR 7% / NVIDIA RTX GPUs / for workstations / Omniverse software / Automotive / 2% of FY24 Revenue / FY24 Revenue $1.1B / 5-YR CAGR 11% / DRIVE Hyperion sensor architecture / with AGX compute / DRIVE AV & IX full stack software / for ADAS, AV & AI cockpit / NVIDIA

**Layout**

Four equal columns of centred content, each with a circular photo at the top, a green category heading, a grey percentage line, a thin divider, revenue and CAGR figures, then product lines. The title is centred horizontally across the top at about 7 percent from the top edge, with a large empty band beneath it before the circles begin at about 24 percent. The NVIDIA wordmark logo sits bottom right at about 95 percent of the height, with a green vertical bar at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Four circular photographs in a row, each about 19.7 percent of slide width and 35.1 percent of slide height, aligned on the same top line. Column 1, Data Center: black-and-white close-up of server racks, with vertical black rack frames, perforated rails and bundled white cables running top to bottom in dense bundles; high contrast, neutral lighting, centred composition, tightly cropped to the circle. Column 2, Gaming: a fantasy forest scene in which a figure draws an ornate bow, with a dark spiked red-and-brown creature body extending across the frame, pine trunks and green ferns behind, and red-orange glowing ground in the lower right; soft hazy daylight, dominant greens with red-orange accents. Column 3, Professional Visualization: a yellow industrial robotic arm with a grey gripper picking a cardboard box with a black logo on it, placed on a grey metal workstation in a grey industrial cell with pipes and racking behind; neutral overhead lighting, dominant grey, white and yellow. Column 4, Automotive: a view from the driver seat over a steering wheel and dashboard, with a heads-up overlay showing a green tag reading 12 mi from destination, circular icons and a 72 degree reading, a green-lit highway road ahead, a speed reading of 65 on the instrument cluster, and a central touchscreen showing a road view with small teal icons; dominant dark grey with teal and green overlay graphics. Each photo is cropped to a circle and sits over a dark semi-transparent circular shape. A thin light grey horizontal divider line runs beneath each percentage line. The NVIDIA logo is a small green and black eye-mark with the NVIDIA wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold black NVIDIA Sans sans-serif, roughly 4.4 percent of slide height. Category headings in medium-weight green #76B900 NVIDIA Sans at about 2.5 percent of slide height. Percentage lines, revenue lines, CAGR lines and product lines in black #000000 NVIDIA Sans, with revenue lines in bold and the rest in regular at about 2.0 percent of slide height. Accent is green #76B900. Photos are perfect circles with no border or shadow. Dividers are thin pale grey lines with a soft fade. No shadows, gradients or corner radii besides the circles.
