### Slide 29

**Text**

Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to / GAAP Financial Measures / 29

**Layout**

Full-bleed background image covering the whole slide with a solid green vertical accent bar at the far left edge, top-aligned, about 1.5% of slide width wide and spanning the top 36% of the height. The title is left-aligned in two lines, starting at about 4.3% from the left edge and vertically centred around 28% to 37% of the slide height, sitting over the white upper-left area of the image. A small page number sits bottom-right at about 91% from the left and 96% from the top.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style 3D rendering, full-bleed. Subject: layered glossy green abstract surfaces shaped like stacked curved chevron or arch panels, sweeping from the lower-right toward the upper-left, with thin bright white-green light streaks running diagonally across the surfaces. Composition: the panels fill the right and bottom two-thirds of the frame, with the darkest recesses at the far right edge; the upper-left quadrant fades to white. Lighting: soft studio light with specular highlights along the panel edges. Dominant colours: bright green (around #76B900 to #8CC61A), pale lime (#D6F5A0) and white (#FFFFFF), with dark green shadows (around #1E4D0A) at the right edge. Not cropped beyond the frame edges, full-bleed, no overlay or tint. Small logo mark at bottom right is a tiny image of about 5.5% by 3.4%, not legible as a distinct logo at this scale.

**Design**

Background: white upper-left fading into a bright green 3D abstract image on the right and bottom. Title text colour black #000000. Accent bar colour green #76B900. Typeface: NVIDIA Sans, a humanist sans-serif, Bold weight for the title and Regular weight for the page number. Title font size about 5.6% of slide height per line, body text not present. Page number about 1.5% of slide height. Shapes: single flat rectangle with square corners, no shadow, no gradient on the bar. Line spacing for the title is tight, about 6% of slide height between baselines.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-bleed 3D rendering of layered glossy green curved panels with white light streaks on a white-to-green gradient background (Covers entire slide, no crop beyond frame, no overlay, background fill #FFFFFF beneath.)
- shape at x 0, y 0, w 1.5, h 36.4 [#76B900]: Vertical green accent bar at left edge (Flat rectangle, square corners, no shadow, no stroke, sits above the background image.)
- title at x 4.3, y 25.8, w 46.7, h 11.4 [NVIDIA Sans, Bold; font size 5.56% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: Reconciliation of Non-GAAP to / GAAP Financial Measures (Two lines, line 1 at x 4.3 y 25.8 w 46.7 h 5.6; line 2 at x 4.3 y 31.8 w 38.1 h 5.6; no italics, no capitals transformation, normal letter-spacing.)

NVIDIA

**Layout**

Single full-width table on a white slide with the title centred above it at the top; the table spans roughly 6.9% to 93.2% of slide width with 6.9% side margins, starting at about 19.3% of height. The seven-column table has a bold black header row and a left-hand row-label column with FY and period labels centred vertically across each pair of rows. Three footnotes (A, B, C) sit left-aligned at the bottom-left margin at about 92.8% to 96.0% of height, and the NVIDIA logo sits bottom-right with a green bar at the right edge. No page number is shown.

**Visuals**

Table with seven columns and fourteen data rows in two-row blocks per period. Header row (black): Operating Income and Margin ($ in Millions & Margin Percentage) | Non-GAAP | Acquisition Termination Cost | Acquisition-Related and Other Costs (A) | Stock-Based Compensation (B) | Other (C) | GAAP. Rows, each period's dollar row then margin percentage row: FY 2020: $3,735 — (31) (844) (14) $2,846 / 34.2% — (0.3) (7.7) (0.1) 26.1%. FY 2021: $6,803 — (836) (1,397) (38) $4,532 / 40.8% — (5.0) (8.4) (0.2) 27.2%. FY 2022: $12,690 — (636) (2,004) (9) $10,041 / 47.2% — (2.5) (7.4) — 37.3%. FY 2023: $9,040 (1,353) (674) (2,710) (79) $4,224 / 33.5% (5.0) (2.5) (10.0) (0.3) 15.7%. FY 2024: $37,134 — (583) (3,549) (30) $32,972 / 61.0% — (1.0) (5.8) (0.1) 54.1%. H1 FY 2024: $10,828 — (311) (1,576) — $8,941 / 52.3% — (1.5) (7.6) — 43.2%. H1 FY 2025: $37,997 — (286) (2,164) 4 $35,551 / 67.8% — (0.5) (3.9) — 63.4%. Thin mid-grey horizontal and vertical rule lines separate all cells. No chart, photograph or icon. Logo: NVIDIA wordmark with eye symbol in black and green at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title and header row use black #000000; body text black #000000; footnotes mid-grey #8C8C8C; the logo and green accent bar use NVIDIA green #76B900. Typeface is NVIDIA Sans (a geometric grotesque, sans-serif) in Bold for the title, Medium for headers and row labels, Regular for numbers, and Italic for footnotes. Title is about 4.4% of slide height; table text about 1.7%; footnotes about 1.2%. Header cells are solid black rectangles with square corners and no shadow; table grid lines are thin light grey (about #BFBFBF, 0.75 pt). No shadows, rounded corners or gradients.

**Layout**

A single full-width data table sits below a centred bold title, with the table spanning from about 6.9% to 93.1% of slide width (equal side margins) and from 19.3% to 87.7% of slide height. The title is centred horizontally at the top (y 7.3%). The header row is a solid black band with white labels; the first column holds row labels and the other four columns hold centred values. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right at about 93% to 99% width and 95% to 98% height, with a green vertical accent bar at the far right edge. There is no footer text and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Table with five columns and eight rows (one header row and seven data rows). Header row (black fills, white text): column 1 '($ in Millions)'; column 2 'Free Cash Flow'; column 3 'Purchases Related to Property and Equipment and Intangible Assets'; column 4 'Principal Payments on Property and Equipment and Intangible Assets'; column 5 'Net Cash Provided by Operating Activities'. Data rows, in order: FY 2020: $4,272 | 489 | — | $4,761. FY 2021: $4,677 | 1,128 | 17 | $5,822. FY 2022: $8,049 | 976 | 83 | $9,108. FY 2023: $3,750 | 1,833 | 58 | $5,641. FY 2024: $26,947 | 1,069 | 74 | $28,090. H1 FY 2024: $8,691 | 537 | 31 | $9,259. H1 FY 2025: $28,418 | 1,346 | 69 | $29,833. Grid: thin mid-grey (about #A6A6A6) horizontal rules between rows and vertical rules between columns, 1 px wide, no fills in the body cells (white). No charts, photographs or icons other than the NVIDIA logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no decoration beyond the table. Title in black (#000000) NVIDIA Sans Bold, sentence-style capitalisation, about 4.4% of slide height. Header labels in white (#FFFFFF) NVIDIA Sans Medium at about 1.7% of slide height; header fill solid black #000000 with no radius. Row labels in black NVIDIA Sans Medium; values in black NVIDIA Sans Regular, same size, centred. Body text is about 1.7% of slide height. Lines are hairline grey, no shadows, no gradients, square corners. Green accent bar at the right edge in NVIDIA green (#76B900).

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 32

**Text**

NVIDIA. / 32

**Layout**

Full-bleed image covering the entire slide with no text block; the NVIDIA logo sits in the upper-left at about 4.5% from the left edge and 3.8% from the top, and the slide number sits at the lower-right near the bottom-right corner (about 91% from the left, 96% from the top). No title, footer or margins beyond the logo and page number.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style 3D rendering filling the full slide: a field of glossy, curved green layered ridges sweeping from the lower-left and centre-right toward the upper-right, forming nested concentric arch shapes that open into a dark recessed tunnel at the right-centre. The upper-left area fades from pure white into pale green streaks radiating diagonally toward the top-right. Lighting is soft and diffuse from the upper-left, producing bright lime highlights on the upper edges of the ridges and deep dark-green shadows inside the recesses. Composition is diagonal, with the bulk of the imagery in the lower-right two-thirds and open white space in the upper-left for the logo. Dominant colours are NVIDIA green (about #76B900 to #8DD300 on the ridges), pale green gradients (about #D9F5A0), dark green shadows (about #1E4D00) and white (#FFFFFF). The image is full-bleed, not cropped, not boxed, with no overlay or tint. The NVIDIA logo is a black eye-shaped mark with a green-outlined square block plus the black wordmark 'NVIDIA' with a registered mark; the logo is black (#000000) on white. The page number is a small black numeral.

**Design**

Background is white fading into pale green on a 3D rendered image; no text block is present, so there is no title or body typography. Logo wordmark is black #000000 in a bold geometric sans-serif (NVIDIA logo wordmark). The page number is set in NVIDIASans Regular, black #000000, about 1.5% of slide height. No shapes, strokes or corner radii; the imagery carries glossy shading with soft gradients.
